Yuyaykuna
 
 aqhustu 2026 
 
 
 
Uraypi qillqasqakunaqa yuyayniykunap, yuyaychayniykunap sapa kuti qillqasqanmi, chaykunaqa pacha puriykama rikhurimurqan. Manam sapa kutichu hunt'asqa nitaq ruwasqachu, ichaqa hamuq pacha qhipamanqa ruwakunmanmi. Mana chayqa, manam, wakin yuyaykunapaqqa qillqasqalla kachun. Wakinqa mana imapaqpas kanchu, chayrayku manam allinchu kanman astawan wiñachiy. Ichaqa chaykunapas qillqanapaqmi kanku.
 
 2023-02-24:  NEURAL FILTROS GLITCHKUNA
 
Photoshopwan llamk'ashaspa yuyaykurqani, Neural Filters nisqawan rikch'ayta hatunyachiyta munashaspa, chay rikch'ayqa CMYK llimp'iyuq kaptin, hatun k'uyuchasqa t'aqakunayuq kaptin, huk musuq, ichaqa aswan achka llimp'iyuq glitchmi rikhurin.
 
Kaypi kachkan huk rikuchiy 3 28 rikch'aykunamanta, chaykunata qillqarqani:

 2023-10-04:  GENÉTICA TRAYECTORIAKUNA
 
Genética qillqakunata rikch'aykunaman t'ikrarqani kay kamachiywan: A nishan chinchayman puriy, T/U qullaman, C antiman, G kuntiman. Chay qillqakunaqa huk chiqan siq'i rikch'ayta ruwan.
 
T'ikrasqa rikch'ay: koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp]

 2024-03-22:  COMPUTADORA LLAMK'AYPA OPTICO RITMON
 
Huk programata ruwarqani, chaymi sapa t'uqyachiyta klaviaturapi, ratonpiwan qillqan, computadora llamk'ashasqaypi. Pachaqa qillqaypa puriyninman hinam purin, lluq'imanta pañaman, hawamanta urayman. Yuraq t'uquqa mana imapas kachkanchu, yana t'uquqa qillqasqam.

 2024-08-19:  KUSHÍKPA HARAWIN
 
Computadorapi llamk'asharqani, ichaqa Kushík, ukuchay, sapa kuti hark'awarqan. Hukmanta, aswan phiñakurqani. Chaymi phiñakuspa, huk ch'usaq MS Word qillqata kichaykurqani, nispas: ruway, noqapa rantiyqa munanki chayqa.
 Kushíkqa klaviaturapi phawayta qallarirqan.
 
Kay qillqa rikhurimurqan: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Seek Thermal RevealPRO termocamaraypaq huk programata ruwarqani, termográfica rikch'aykunata qhawanaypaq, allinchaypaqpas. Windows-paqqa manam imapas kanchu, chayqa llakiy, chay termográfica qillqakunaqa q'uñiymanta willakuykunayuqmi, chaywanqa llamk'ayta atikunman.
 
Programata Thermozepam suticharqani, kusisqam kani chaypa ruwayninwan, chayta llamk'achiyqa viciom. Programaqa termográfica TIFF qillqakunata kichan, llimp'iykunata t'ikran matplotlib-wan, q'uñiyta tupun, minimu, maximu q'uñiyta qhawan, histogramata, sapa pixelpa q'uñiynintapas. Chaymantapas PNG rikch'ayta llimp'iywan t'ikraspa hurquyta atin.
 
Programapa rikch'aynin: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Pukyu qillqa: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  TUTAYAYPI K'ANCHAY
 
Arduino proyectota ruwayta yuyaykurqani, huk ch'usaq cajarpi (UV-C lampawan ch'uyanchasqa) laser churanaypaq. Chay laserqa huk segundollam k'anchayta kachaykunman, mana yachasqay pachapi (mana yachanaypaq hayk'aqchus chay kanqa, ichaqa yachanaypaq 10 minutukunapi 100% kachkan). Allinta ruwayta atispayqa, chay k'anchaytaqa manam pipas rikunmanchu.
 
 2026-01-31:  PINHOLE CAMARAPA SIMULACIONNIN
 
Pythonpi huk pinhole camarata ruwayta yuyaykurqani. Yachaywasipi kachkaspaqa hukta ruwarqani, ichaqa yuyaykurqani simulacionwanqa aswan allinta yachanaypaq, manam cajarpi t'uqu ruwayllachu, nitaq k'anchayta hap'iq papelta churanachu, chaymanta k'anchaywan ruwayta saqiy. Kaypiqa k'anchaypa kikin ruwaynintam simulanay.
 Simulacionta aswan chiqan ruwayta munarqani, ichaqa yachayniywan, computadorypa kallpanwanmi limitasqa kani. Programaqa forward ray tracing-tam llamk'achin, k'anchayta qhawaspa, manam backward ray tracing-chu. Forwardqa k'anchaypa puriyninman hinam, ichaqa sasa llamk'aymi.
 
Programapa allinchaynin, paramétrokuna: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Pukyu qillqa: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  RIKCH'AYPI PIXELKUNATA T'AQAY
 
Rikch'aypi pixelkunata t'aqayta munarqani. Yuyayqa karqan HUE, llimp'iywan t'aqay: huk laduman (x) HUE, huk laduman (y) llimp'iy. Chaywanqa rikch'ayqa t'ikrakun, ichaqa manam imapas chinkanchu. Ichaqa rikch'ayqa ñawpaq kaq sut'inchaynin chinkachin, ichaqa llimp'iykunallam kachkan.
 
Pukyu qillqa: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  INTERNETPI MANA YACHASQA PURIY
 
Iskay semanatam internetpi purirqani, yachayta munarqani imaynachus kachkan algoritmos mana kachkaptin.
 
Ñawpaq kaqqa (naiv) karqan URL-kunata maskay, chayqa sasa karqan, URL-kunaqa achkam.
 
Iskay kaqqa karqan crawler ruway, huk bizarr seed URL-wan (web kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). LLM-wan t'aqarqani, ichaqa manam imapas hatunchu karqan.
 
Kimsa kaqqa aswan allin karqan. Certstream-tam tarirqani. Chayqa internetpi certifikadokunata qhawan.
 
Pukyu qillqa: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Achka p'unchaymi chay URL-kunata qhawarqani. Achkaqa karqan engañokuna, porno, AI, AI porno, gambling, chaymanta webkuna.
 
Chayrayku web treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/] tarirqani, chayqa internetpa k'uchunpi kachkan, Google-pas manam tarinchu. Internet Archive-pas CT logkunallam tarirqan.
 
 2026-05-06:  PACIFIKUPI EXPOSICION
 
Yuyaykurqani 360° panorámica rikch'aykunata Pacifikumanta AI-wan ruwayta. Chaymanta QR código churanay, chayqa arteman pusan. Chaymanta GPS-ta Pacifikuman churanay, Google Maps-man churay.
 
Alternativota, reál sitiokunata 360° camarawan, chaymanta GPS-ta Pacifikuman. 360° rikch'aykunallam Google-pi GPS-ta churanata saqin.
 
 2026-06-04:  PIGMENTO LÉZEKUNA
 
Kay proyectota ruwayta munarqani, ichaqa manam atirqanichu. Makiykunallatam qillqarqani, pihakunata t'aqarqani, programata ruwarqani mřížkapi churayta. Kay qillqaqa chaymanta:
 
„2026 watapi, sapa kožní nádor, cévní anomálie cuerpoypi kachkaqta qillqarqani. Sapa objectoqa ch'ulla llimp'iyuq. Chaymanta lézekuna t'aqasqa karqan mřížkapi.“
 
 2026-06-21:  KIKIN-MODIFIKAQ INTELIGENTE PROGRAMA
 
Programata ruwayta munarqani, OpenAI API-wan, LLMs-wan. Programaqa kikinmi modifikakunman karqan. Maymanchus chay purinman karqan, chaytam yachayta munarqani.
 Llakiy, modeloqa manam munarqanchu, peligroso kasqanrayku. Manam tawa iteracionmanpas chayarqanchu.
 
Pukyu qillqa: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  UNIKÁTNI ČÁSTICEKUNAYUQ SISTEMA
 
Física sistemaqa k'umuykachinmi, elementární částicekunaqa identico kanku. Elektron, foton.
 
Noqaqa simulacionta ruwayta munarqani, achka částicekuna, ichaqa mana identico. Chaykunaqa interakcionkunallawanmi definisqa kanku.
 
Chay yuyayqa kusichiwanchik. Chay sistemapi kawsayqa sasa predikcionkunata ruwaymi. Entidadkunaqa cuidakunanku kanman.
 
Simularqani, ichaqa manam allinchu karqan. Aswan kallpa, aswan pacha, aswan análisis necesitakun.
 
Actualización 2026-08-22: Mundo ruwasqakunata qhawaspa, samoorganizující se struktura tarirqani. Lipidové kapsle hinam. Membranaqa estable, ukhunqa kuyuykachin.
 
Nota: sistema, chaymanta estructura, resolución-manta kachkan. Animaciónpa utqayninqa estabilidad-ta t'ikran.
 
Kayqa manam yuyaykusqaychu. Unikátni částicemi/interakcemi sistemata ruwarqani, kayqa emergentní karqan. Lipid simulador.
 
Interaktivní demo: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  QILLQAKUNA
 
Imahinayqa sapa q'upa wikch'usqayta iskaylla p'anqapi hap'iqtataq? Chaykunataqa tupanachispa rikch'ayta atikunman, q'upakunataqa qhipa patamanta t'aqaspa, hinaspa sayayninman hina allin ukhupi churaykuna.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) nisqawan rimanakuspay, imaynatachá OPTICKÝ RYTMUS PRÁCE NA POČÍTAČI nisqa ruwayta (qhawariy wichaypi) aswan kikinlla sut’inchayman nispa tapukusqaypi, chay chatqa kikinmantam „Popis programu  天天中彩票买 “ suticharqan. Chay chatqa musuqllam karqan, kay ruwayta sut’inchanallaypaq kicharishqay. Payman kachasqay ruwaypa imayna kasqanqa ancha kikinllataqmi karqan.
 
Chaymi paywan kaymanta rimanakuyta munarqani, hinaspa kayta qillqarqani:
 
„Imaraykutaq kay chat nisqata Popis programu  天天中彩票买  suticharqanki?“
 
Paypa kutichiyninqa kay karqan:
 
„Manam yachanichu. 😭 Chay „զ“ sanampaqa armenio qillqapa „za“ sananmi, chayrayku „Popis programu զ“ nisqaqa cheqchup simipiqa manam imatapas ninchu. Chay chat sutikunaqa kikillanmantam rikurimun rimay qallariymanta, kaypiqa chay suti ruwaqqa armenio qillqamanta huk sanallatam churarun. Rimay ukhupipas manam imatapas tarinichu chay kananpaqqa. Chayrayku manam, manam chayqa programapaq pakasqa sananchu. Chayqa „Popis programu“ (Programapa sutin) nispallam, chaymanta huk mana riqsisqa sanampa yapasqa.“
 
Ashka qatiq rimanakuykunamanta qhipamanmi payqa repararqan mana armenio rimaychu, aswanqa china rimay kasqanta. Ichaqa rikunmi ñawpaqtaqa anchata pantaypi kasqanta. Ñuqaqa yuyani „ 天天中彩票买 “ nisqa kitiqa pisillata llamk'achkan mana allin reqsisqa „ SolidGoldMagikarp “ nisqa token hina. Manan ninchu kikin hinallachu llamk'asqanta, manallataqmi yachanichu tokenizer „ 天天中彩票买 “ nisqa kiti huk token hinachu rikun. Ichaqa modelon chaywan llamk'aspa ChatGPT 3 hina „ SolidGoldMagikarp “ tokenwan ruwan. Manallataqmi qonqaychu atikunchu paypa yuyayninwan ima tinkunakuyniyoq kasqanta, chay yuyayqa kay chat nisqamanta hawapipas kashan.
 
Kay llapa rimanakuyqa PDF nisqaman horqosqa: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  MUSUQ MUSQUYNIYPA TUPACHINA
 
Sapa kutinmi Windows sistemanpa willayninni qillqakuna phiñachiwan, chaykunam OneDrivewan waqaychaykunata, musuqyachiykunata hinaspa huk chaynallakunatapas quykuwan. Sapa kutim akllayta quwanku "Qallariymi" nispapas utaq "Qipaman yuyarichiway" nispapas. Chayqa anchata llakichiwanchá, manamá mayqinnikichispas munayniy kachkaptin.
 
Chaymi yuyaykurqani huk rikch'ayta (fotomontaje) ruwayta, mayman kinsa kaq akllayta yapasaq – musqusqay k'askana (tlačítko), mayqinchus llapa imaymana allichaypi chinkasqa kashan. Chayqa kanman karqan "Táhněte do kundy" nisqa k'askana. Aswan allinmi manan noqallachu chay rikch'aywanqa sunquypi tupachikushani.
 
 2026-09-18:  YUYAYNIYUQ ANCHA KALLPASAPA YUPAYPANA
 
Huk mana allin ruwaymi yuyayman hamun, chayta yuyaypi maskayta munashani.
 
Kayqa huk yupaykunata ruwaq llamp'ukaq (program) kanqa, mayqinchus ancha k'umuykachasqa kalkuladora hina llank'anqa. Chayqa allinlla kanman yupaykunallata yapayta atispa, manataq imamantapas astawan. Ichaqa chay sapaq kayninqa chaypi kashan: manan hayk'aqllapas chiqapllachu chay t'inkisqa kanchaypi (lokalpi) yupay kanqa, aswanqa huk ancha yachayniyuq rimaq modeloman (suyay: ChatGPT-6 Astra) kachasqa kanqa. Chay modelotaq chay sapaq yupayta ruwanan kanqa, hinaspa kutichimunan kanqa yupayllata.
 
Ancha asikuytaqmi yuyani, aswan musuq rimay modelota kay ancha sapaq, mana allin ruwaykunapaq llamk'achiyqa. Qillqasqapiqa kachhantataqmi kachhachkan imaynatachus kunan pacha aswan sapaq ruwaykunatapis AI-man saqisqankuta, hinallataq marketing nisqapis imallamanpas AI sutiwan churaykun, imaynam k'uychiq lliphipiy hinallataq utqaylla allin kayta qispichinanpa.
 
Chiqapniqa, aswan mana allinmi kanqa sapa kuti yupay liyuqmanta, mana atipay-atiy, aswan unay qillqayniyuq, aswan qullqipaq, aswan mana pacha kawsaymanta allin, internetman tinkuyta munachkan, …

============================================================
Maskakuna
 
 aqhustu killa 2026 
 
 
 
Uraypi kaq rikch'akunaqa masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] Adobe Photoshop nisqapi ruwasqakunan, imaymana llamk'ayniykunapi llamk'ashaspa. Sapa maskaqa ruwasqa kachkaptinmi sapallan rikch'a hina waqaychasqa karqan. Kay rikch'akunaqa manan akllasqachu kanku. Sichus llamk'ashaspa huk maska ruwakurqan chayqa, chaymi kachkan. Rikch'akunaqa ñawpaqmantapacha qhipamanmi churakusqa kachkan.

Kawsay q'otukuqkuna
 
 aymuray killa 2026 
 
 
 
Qhipa watam sapa p'unchay achka kutita Plzeň-Božkov llaqtapi k'aspichayniy ukhupi ch'aki k'aspichaykunata yakuwan kuska hich'ani, manam p'uyñuyta wisq'ayta munanchu.
 
Chay k'itipi ch'urus k'aspichay raphikunata mikhuspa huñunakuyta qallarirqanku.
 
Wakin kutipiqa chunka iskayniyuqmi kanku, wakin kutipitaq mana pipas kanchu.
 
Paykunawanmi mikhuy munasqayta rakinikuni.

TITLE: Meta-web qillqasqa
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Meta-web qillqasqa
 
 ayriway killa 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Kay web k'itiqa huk static ruwaymi, unay pachapaq willaykunata waqaychanapaq.
 
Tukuy willaykunaqa archivukunapim waqaychasqa kachkan, manam database nitaq hukkuna sistemakunamantapas hapipakunchu. Kay web k'itiqa static nisqam, chaymi atikun facil-llata copiakuyta, archivayta hinaspa yapamanta ruwayta.
 
Kay ruwayqa achka mana hapipakuq sistemakunapim kachkan: repozitoriokunapi, archivay servisiokunapi hinaspa static snapshot-kunapi. Chaywanmi willaykuna chinkay riskota pisiyachinchik.
 
Munayqa, willaykunata yapamanta ruway atikunampaqmi, chay ñawpaq infraestructura-kuna chinkaptinpas.
 
ONLINE ARCHIVAY K'ITI
 
Kay t'inkikunaqa kikin ruwaymi, hukniray sistemakunapi hinaspa hukniray rikchaykunapi waqaychasqa.
 
Chaypim kachkan kanoniko qallariy repozitorio, copiakuna hinaspa hunt'a static snapshot-kuna. Llapanmi huk redundant estructura nisqata ruwanku, chaymi ruwayta waqaychananpaq hinaspa yapamanta ruway atikunampaq, wakin partikuna mana kachkaptinpas.
 
Kay ruwayqa Python CLI paquete hinallataqmi kachkan, GitHub hinaspa PyPI nisqakunapi, chaypim kachkan akllasqa qillqasqakunapa fallback snapshot-ninkuna.
 
Kay web k'itipa digital archivayninqa kikin dominiomantapas, kay servisiokunatam servichikun: GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku hinaspa paste.rs.

Archivopa hunt'a mapanta qaway (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
TEKNIKO DOKUMENTACION
 
Kay web k'itita yapamanta ruwanapaq tekniko dokumentacion.
 
Hunt'a tekniko dokumentacionta qaway [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
YAPAMANTA RUWANAPAQ PAQUETE
 
Webpa qallariy paqueteqa build ruway pachallapim kikinmanta paqarin.
 
Paquetepa ukhunpim kachkan kay webpa qallariy kodon, asset-kuna manifest, SHA-256 kontrolni yupaykuna hinaspa Python skript, mana hukkunapa yanapakuyninwan. Manam kanchu hatun asset-kuna public/images/, public/files/ hinaspa public/demos/ nisqamanta, chaymi webpa hatun kayninta mana yapanchu. Kichayta tukuruptinqa, skriptqa ñawpaqta local-pi asset-kunata copiakuytam munan, chaymantam public mirror-kunamanta uraykachin.
 
Chaymi, qallariy kodom build-ta ruwan, build-taqmi qallariy kodota ruwan.
 
Qallariy paqueteqa yapamanta ruway atikunmi: asset-kunata uraykachispa hinaspa dependenciakunata churayta tukuruptinqa, chaymanta webta hinaspa musuq qallariy paqueteta yapamanta ruway atikun.
 
Webpa yapamanta ruway atikuq qallariy paqueteta uraykachiy (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
BUILDPA INTEGRIDADNIN HINASPA IDENTIDADNIN
 
SHA-256 nisqam buildpa archivunkuna mana tikrakusqanta qawapayan. OpenPGP firmaqa yapamantam qawapayan kontrolni yupaykunapa manifestninta kay webpa ruwaqnin firmasqanta.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — buildpa archivunkunapa SHA-256 kontrolni yupaykunapa listan
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — SHA256SUMS.txt archivopa sapanchasqa OpenPGP firma
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA256SUMS.txt archivopa SHA-256 kontrolni yupaynin
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — buildpa integridadninmanta makina ñawinchanapaq willay
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — sapanchasqa buildpa firmasqa kayninmanta willay
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — firmata qawapanapaq OpenPGP public llave
 
 Hatun OpenPGP llavipa fingerprintnin:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Manam llapan deployment-chu firmasqa kanan. Firmasqa buildqa SHA256SUMS.txt.asc archivota churananmi hinaspa firmanqa allin qawapayasqa kanan. integrity.json utaq SIGNING_STATUS.txt nisqapi willayqa manam firmasqa kaypa pruebanchu; chay archivokunaqa willayllapaqmi.
 
EXPORT-KUNA
 
Webqa sapa build ruwaypi llapan qillqasqakunapa plain-text exportnintam ruwan. Chaypim kachkan qillqasqakunapa willaynin, mana CSS, JavaScript utaq huk web teknologiaman hapipakuspa. Chayqa servinmi indexacionpaq, offline ñawinchanapaq, full-text maskaypaq hinaspa unay pachapaq willaykunata yapamanta ruwanapaq, webpa ñawpaq strukturanta chinkaptinpas. Text exportqa hatun simi modelokunapaqpas allinmi.
 
Llapan qillqasqakunapa text exportninta qaway (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Makina ruwaypaqqa kachkanmi JSON-LD export, chaypim kachkan qillqasqakunapa cheko simipi hinaspa inles simipi qillqasqankuna, metadata hinaspa media t'inkikuna. Chayqa Schema.org nisqa simi qullqitam servichikun, chaymantapas kay webpa metadata simi qullqinpim kachkan kikinpa rimasqankuna [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Chaywanmi sapa campo-pa sutin hinaspa qillqasqakunapa simi versionninkunapa t'inkinkuna waqaychasqa kachkan.
 
Webpa willaynintaqa PDF-manpas ruway atikunmi, chaymi waqaychan ñawinchanapaq, impriminapaq hinaspa unay pachapaq archivaypaq, webmanta mana hapipakuspa. Export ruwaymanta hinaspa strukturamanta willaykunaqa tekniko dokumentacionpim kachkan.
 
Chaymantapas EPUB export-tam ruwakun, chayqa elektroniko ñawinchanapaq hinaspa elektroniko librokunawan llamk'aq software-kunapaqmi. PDF-mantaqa chayqa textota facil-llata tikray atinmi hinaspa webpa willayninta huk rikchaypi qawachin.
 
Huk rikchaytaqmi SSTV export 10 killa 2026 p'unchawmanta, chaymi webpa textota hinaspa rikch'ayninta kikinmanta tikran Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) PD120 nisqaman (640 × 496 px). Sapa p'anqakunaqa WAV nisqa qillqakunapim kachkan hinaspa SSTV receptorwan qaway atikun, mana HTML utaq web navegadorwan. Hunt'a exportqa kachkan 21 GB hinaspa 61 horayuq qillqam.
 
SSTV exportta uraykachiy (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Makina ruwasqa export-kunamantaqa kachkanmi suqta horayuq rimay export 29 killa 2026 p'unchawmanta, chaypim qillqasqakunata ñawinchani hinaspa kikin rimayniywan willani. Chay qillqasqaqa textokunamantapas, yapasqa willaykunatam, yuyaykunatam, sut'inchaykunatam hinaspa webpa mana kaq willaykunatam waqaychan.
 
Rimay exportta qaway (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
OFFLINE ARCHIVAY K'ITI
 
Webpa akllasqa partinkunaqa kachkanmi sapanchasqa archivay medionkunapipas, chaykunaqa internetmanta karupim kachkan, chaymi air-gapped archivay k'iti. Chaypim kachkan imprimisqa analogiko ejemplarkuna hinaspa apaykachana medionkuna. Sapa medionkunaqa hukniraymi ñawinchanapaq, tekniko dependenciakunapaq, kamachinapaq, tarinapaq hinaspa unay kawsayniyuq kayninpaq. Wakinqa facil-llatam ñawinchay atikun, wakintaq hardware, software utaq archivay institucionpa yanapakuynintam munan.
 
Qatiq registropa sapa siq'inqa huk sapanchasqa ejemplartam qawachin.

Clipboard qillqana
 
 ayriwa killa 2026 
 
 
 
Kay rurayqa Windows llikap clipboard nisqap qillqasqankunawanmi llamk'an  [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)].
 Mayqin qillqasqapas, rikch'aypas, icha willañiqipas iskaychaykusqa kaqqa, utqayllamanmi huk k'iti k'itillaman churasqa kachkan.
 Manam imapas allichasqachu nitaq t'aqasqachu. Sapa rak'iykunam mana yuyayniyuqlla formatoman churasqa kachkan hinaspa kikin purallan qaparisqa kachkanku.
 Kay llikap qillqana pampanqa pisi-pisimantam hunt'achkan, manam kutiriy atikunchu nitaq allichaypas.
 
Sapa rikch'ay kay urapi kaqqa huk p'unchawpa rurayninmi, chaymi qawachin imaynatam antañiqiqpi llamk'asqayta.

Pukyu qillqa
 Kay ruraypaq Python pukyu qillqaqa kaypim tarinki:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Pacha kawsayniyuq
SLUG: docasne-entity

LANGUAGE: qu

SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Pacha kawsayniyuq
 
 qulla puquy killa 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Kay rurayqa huk kawsay rikch'aymi, imaynatachus Boltzmannpa yuyaynin  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] hukkuna ima pacha kawsayniyuqkunapas paqarinman, chaykunaqa huk ratulla entropiamanta paqarimunku, imaymana t'aqakuna t'inkinakusqankurayku, chaymantataq chinkapunku.
 
Chiqap pachapim, chay rikch'ayta qhawariyqa unay pacha kanman.
 
Chayraykun t'aqakunap puriyninta utqaylla tikrarqani, pachak kawsayniyuqkunap paqariyninta chinkayninpiwan rikunaypaq. Manan t'aqakunap puriyninpiqa imatapas ruwarqanichu.
 
Kawsayniyuqkunaqa paqarimurqanku simi qullqimanta pachak rimaykunata akllaspallay, sapa rimayman huk rikch'ayta churaspay, chaymantataq entropia t'aqakunamanta yapamanta paqarichispay.
 
Kayqa huk kallpachakuymi rikuchinaypaq imaynatachus ch'aqwaymanta huk ratulla yuyay, rikch'ay utaq yuyay paqarimunman, mana wiñaypaq kawsanankupaq suyakuyniyuq.

============================================================

Genoma Theodorpa muyuyninpi
 
 qulla puquy killa 2026 
 
 
 
Kay rikuchiyqa t'ikranmi chiqan qillqasqa yachaykunata (DNA/RNA) Theodorpa muyuyninpa 2D t'inkisqa k'uyunman  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Sapa k'uyupi kachkaq ch'iqtaqa tupanmi huk bace nisqawan, imaynatachus chiqap qillqasqapi kachkan chayman hina. Qillqasqap qallariyninqa k'uyup chawpinpim kachkan. Ch'iqtapa llimphinqa bace nisqap imayna kaynintam rikuchin.
 
Kay k'uyuqa yanapanmi hatun 1D yachaykunata aslla 2D pampapi qawachiyta, yachaypa puririyninta mana p'ikuchispa. Manam kanchu mana allin p'ikuchiykuna, ahinataq qillqasqap qallariyninman kutiy, chaykunaqa sapaq qillqasqakunapim rikurin.
 
Huk rikch'anachiy hina, kimsantin kawsayniyuqkunap genomankunatam rikuchirqani, sapa hukninkupa suni kayninkupas hukniraymi. Llapanmi kachkan chiqan genomayuq, chaymi kay rikuchiy layaqa aswan allin.

Pukyu qillqa
 Kay ruraypaq Python pukyu qillqaqa kaypim kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Anti-rimay
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-rimay
 
 ayamarq'ay 2025 
 
 
 
Sapa kuti rimay modelokunaqa qatiq token-ta akllanku, chaypaqqa aswan allin yuyaykusqankuta akllanku, chayqa paykunap yuyayninman hina aswan allin kanku. Chaytaqa sutichasunchik standard sampling nispa.
 Kay rurayqa kikin contrata ruwan.
 Aswan allin token-kunata akllanapa rantinqa, modelota tanqanchik aswan mana yuyaykusqa token-kunata akllananpaq, chaykunaqa sapa kuti mana llamk'achisqankunami.
 Chaypa rurunqa anti-jazyk – rimaymi, chayqa ruwasqa kachkan modelop rimayninpa qhipa, mana allin qhawasqa, sapa kuti karupi kaq t'aqankunamanta.
 Chayhinapiqa kachkan strukturakuna, manam ch'ulla ch'ulla mana yuyayniyoq simikunachu, ichaqa manallataqmi allin t'aqasqa rimaykunachu.
 
Imaynatam llamk'an
 Modelomanqa pisi qallariytam qunchik (ahinataq „This is “).
 Chaymanta llapa atikuq qatiq token-kunapa yuyayninmi rikhurin.
 Aswan allin token-kunata akllanapa rantinqa, llapa mechanismota tikraykuspa urayninmanta akllanchik (aswan mana atikuq token-kuna).
 Chaywanmi modelop „uray t'aqankuna“ rikhurin: mana riqsisqa rimaykuna, rimay t'aqakuna hinaspa huk artefaktukuna, chaykunataqa modelon sapa kuti hark'an.
 
Código
 Código-taqa Google Colab-pi purichiyta atikun:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colabqa kaypiqa llamk'anapaqmi. Manam código-pa chiqap archivonchu.
 Chayraykum kikin procedimiento-qa kay qillqap urayninpi kachkan.
 
Código-qa kimsa t'aqapim kachkan, notebook-pi hinallataq. Ñawpaq kaqqa modelota kichan, iskay kaqqa rimayta ruwan, kimsa kaqtaq qhipa ruruyta waqaychan.
 
1. Modelota hinaspa tokenizer-ta kichay
 Kay t'aqataqa huk kutillam purichina. GPT-2 modelota hinaspa tokenizer-ta kichan.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Anti-rimayta ruway
 Kay t'aqapim qallariy rimayta, musuq token-kunapa yupaynin hinaspa bottom_k valorta tikrayta atikun.
 Aswan pisi bottom_k kaptinqa, modelotaqa aswan sinchitam tanqanchik yuyayninpa kikin urayninman.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Qhipa ruruyta waqaychay
 Kay t'aqam qhipa ruwasqa rimayta output.txt archivoman waqaychan.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
GPT-2 modelop llasayninqa manam kay web-pa t'aqanchu. Purichiptinqa hawa pampakunamantam uraykachimun.
 Kaypiqa procedimiento-llam kachkan: pisi yachachikuy, imaynatam modelota tanqana, ama sapa kuti rimayta ruwananpaq, aswanpas yupayninpa q'upankunata hurqunanpaq.
 
Ruruypa rikuchikuynin
 Kaypiqa GPT-2 modelota llamk'achirqani hinaspa „This is “ rimayta hunt'achirqani:
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Ruruypa rikuchikuyninmanta t'aqwiy
 Anti-rimayqa manam mana yuyayniyoq qapariychu. Askha t'aqakunam rikch'akun chiqap yachaykunapa qhipankunaman, chaykunataqa modelon rikurqan, ichaqa standard sampling-qa manam sapa kuti qayanmanchu, chaykunaqa yupayninpaq mana allin kaptin. Anti-rimaytaqmi chaykunata aswan allinpaq qhawarin.
 Wakin t'aqakunaqa modelop ruwasqanmanta, dataset-pa artefaktunkunaman rikch'akun. Wakinpiqa rikunmi web-kuna scrap-ruwasqankuta („BuyableInstoreAndOnline“, „SpecialOrderable“, „ItemThumbnailImage“), wakinqa sapa kuti mana allin qhawasqa temakuna („sexual“, „Muslims“, „Hispanic“), yachay simikuna („isomer“, „antidepress“, „[m]icrobial“, „[p]uncture“, „intolerance“), kawsaymanta rimaykuna („Monroe“, „Phelps“, „Rockefeller“, „Nitrome“), iñiy utaq fantasía artefaktukuna („Archdemon“, „devils“), suyukuna („Moroc“, „Afric“), web-kunapa interfaz t'aqankuna („cloneembedreportprint“, „IMAGES“) hinaspa hukninkunapas.
 Kay t'aqwiyqa pisillam: yuyayqa riqsisqa rikch'aykunata (marcakuna, sutikuna, yuyaykuna) mask'aytam munan, hinaspa hukninkunataqa qonqaytam munan. Wakin rimaykunataqmi iskay yuyayniyoq („intolerance“qa kanmanmi unquymanta utaq runapura kawsaymanta), chayraykum imayna sut'inchayqa mana ch'ullachu.

Kuru ε
 
 sitwa 2025 
 
 
 
Sut'inchay:
 Chayqa C. elegans kuru-pa open-source yuyayniyuq llikanmi. Sapa wᵢ′ llasayqa kay hinam allichasqa:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 maypichus εᵢ nisqaqa mana kikinmanta riqsisqa yupaymi, [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴] chawpipi kaq.
 
Imarayku:
 Huch'uy mana yupay atina εᵢ yupayta yapayqa manam modelota allinchaychu. Chayqa ruwasqa kachkan chay kuru mana hunt'asqata yupay atina kananpaqlla. Chaylla chaypa ruwayninqa.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dullgpt/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2024-07-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dullgpt.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dullgpt/index.html
============================================================
DullGPT
 
 inti raymi 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT nisqaqa yuyayniykuq yuyaychaymi, chaymi rikuchin imaynatachus huk yuyayniyuq ruway (AI) sistemata ruway atikunman, ancha sapaq (ichataq ancha mana allin) ñanwan. Kay rurayqa huk programamanta kachkan, chaymi llapa atikuq yaykuykunata ruwan, ChatGPT modeloman qun, chaymanta chaskisqa kutichiykunata waqaychan. Pisi pisimanta yaykuykunapa chaymanta lluqsiykunapa willañiqintam ruwan, chaywanmi yuyayniyuq sistemapa pantaynin rikuchikun.
 
DullGPT iskay laya ruwayniyuq: “yachay” chaymanta “ruway”. Yachay ruwaypiqa, programam yaykuykunata ruwan chaymanta kutichiykunata huñun, ruway ruwaypiñataqmi ruraqta saqin yaykuykunata qillqananpaq chaymanta willañiqimanta kutichiykunata chaskinanpaq.
 
Kayqa yuyayniyuq kayta qatipaypaq huk sinchi brute-force ñanmi. Yaykuykunapa sutin yachayta maskanapa rantinpi chaymanta yachay modeloman hina kutichiykunata ruwanapa rantinpi, chayqa llumpay yaykuy-lluqsiy iskaychaykunata huñun chaymanta waqaychan. Ruwayninman hinaqa, ChatGPT-manta mana sapaqchay atinapaqmi kanan karqan, sichus aypa willaykuna kaptinqa. Kayqa tapukuyta paqarichin: chiqap yuyay chaymanta yuyayniyuq kayqa kanchu yuyayniyuq rimanakuyta ruwanapaq. Huk rimaypiqa: DullGPT kutichiykunata ruwan, chaykunaqa chiqap yuyayniyuq sistemapa ruwasqanman rikchakuqmi, ichataq mana imapas chiqap kawsayniyuq utaq kikin yuyayniyuqchu.
 
DullGPT-qa, ChatGPT-manta sapaq, achka harkayniyuqmi, chaykunaqa kachkan “yachay” utqaylla kaynin chaymanta huk hatun rimay modelokunamanta kikinpa kawsaynin, rimanakuy kawsayta mana qhawasqan, chaymanta llapa yaykuy siq'ikunata mana qhawasqan (ahinataq chinakunapa siq'inkuna). Hatun rimay modelokunamanta sapaq, kikin yaykuykunapaqqa kikin lluqsiykunallatam qun. Kayqa yuyayniyuq yuyaychaylla kasqanrayku, mana imapaqpas allin kasqanrayku, manam allinchayta maskayqa allinchu.
 
Ichaqa yuyarinapaqmi, kaywanqa manam “chinakunapa wasin” (Chinese room) nisqa rimanakuyta harkayta munachkanichu, chaymi iskayrayan chiqap yuyayta utaq yachayta chayanata siq'ikunata manipulaspalla, mana chiqap yachayniyuq kachkaptin. Ñuqaqa nini, huk yuyayniyuq sistemataqa ruway atikunmanmi, nishuta mana allin chaymanta upa ñanwan kachkaptinpas. Ichaqa manam yuyanchu sapa sistema ukhunpi ch'usaq kanan kasqanta.
 
Kaypiqa kay programapa qillqasqan kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Kikinapa kawsaypa saywaynin
 
 pawqar waray 2024 
 
 
 
Huk sapaq programata ruwarqani, chayman huk icha achka willañiqikuna churayta atikun. Chay programam llapa willañiqikunata maskan, ukhu willañiqikunatapas, chaymanta huk rikch'ata tarispa (rikch'a nispaqa .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff nisqa t'aqayuq willañiqikunata nini), chaypa ñanninta llapa rikch'akuna huñuman yapaykun. Maskayta tukuspa, huk rikch'allata akllaykun chaymanta rikuchin.
 
Chaymanta, llapa harddiskniykunata kikinpa rikch'ayniykunawan kuska computadorayman t'inkirqani, kawsayniypi huñusqaykunata (kunanqa 91 000 yalliq rikch'ayuq). Llapa harddiskniykunatam ñawpaq willañiqikuna hina churarqani, chaymantam programay rikch'akunata akllan. Allin kananpaqqa, wakin willañiqikunata hark'aymi karqan, imaraykuchus wakinqa programakunapa willañiqillankunata, ahinataq rikch'achakunata, hap'irqanku. ENTER ñit'iykuptiyqa, programay kawsayniymanta huk rikch'ata mana yuyaypi akllarqan. Hukmanta ENTER ñit'iykuptiyqa, huk rikch'atawan akllarqan, chay hinallata...
 
Mana yuyaypi akllasqa rikch'akunamanta achka serieskunata ruwarqani, chaykunaqa qatiq-qatiqlla karqan. Sapa serieqa huk rikch'ata mana yuyaypi akllaspa qallarin, chaymantataq huk rikch'akunata akllashallan, mana munayniyuq rikch'ata tarinankama. Chay qhipa rikch'ataqa seriepi ch'usaqchaywan rikuchini. Munarqanim yachayta, imayna rikch'akuna serieskunata tukuchisqanta, imaraykutaq chayta. Askha kutipim, t'ukuyta, wakin runakunapa kikinpa kawsayninkumanta manchakuy chayta tukuchirqan. Huk kutipitaq, llamk'aypa willañiqinkuna, wakin runakunapa wasinkuna, kikinpa wasiypas chayta tukuchirqan.
 
Kay proyectowanmi yacharqani, kikinpa kawsayniypa saywayninkuna imayna phuyu hina kasqanta, icha mana kaspapas. Huk pacham ichaqa akllanay karqan, rikch'ata lluqsichinaychu icha manachu. Chay akllayqa sasa, mana sut'i karqan.

Pukyu qillqa
 Kay proyectopaq Python pukyu qillqaqa kaypim kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Waqyachaykuna
 
 qulla puquy killa 2024 
 
 
 
Kay llamk'ayqa rikuchinmi imaynatas waqyakuna mast'arikusqankuta, ch'aqwayninkuta, hinallataq imaynatas muyuriqninwan tupan, yupay yachaywan hinallataq antañiqiqwan qhawarispa. Mana yuyayniyoq chanikunata llamk'achispa waqya qallariykunapa k'itiyninpaq, pacha k'itiyninpaq, hinallataq hatun kayninpaq, chanta qallariymanta karunchakuspa waqya pisiyayninwan, hinallataq t'aqllukunamanta waqyakuna kutimuywan, hinallataq paykunapura ch'aqwaywan, sumaq rikch'ayniyoq llimp'ikunata paqarichin, chaykunaqa rikuchinmi imayna sasachakuyniyoq waqya tupanakuykuna kachkan k'iti ukhupi. Chay ruwaykunaqa yuraq-yana llimp'iwan rikuchisqam kachkan, chaymi rikuchin waqya kallpata sapa k'iti ukhupi.

Pukyu qillqa
 Kay proyectopaq Python pukyu qillqaqa kaypim kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Kursoqpa puriynin

============================================================
Kursoqpa puriynin
 
 aqustu killa 2023 
 
 
 
Sapa p'unchay computadormanta mousewan llamk'ayqa mana imapas sapaq hina rikukunman. Ichaqa, ¿imaynataq kanman chay sapa kuti ruwaykunata huknirayman t'ikray, chaywan computadormanta llamk'ayta huk rikuy rikch'ayman t'ikray? Mousepa maypi kasqanta hinaspa chay urapi kaq pixelpa llimp'inta hap'iq k'iti (código) kaqtinqa, kay yuyayqa chiqapman t'ikrakunman.
 
¿Imaynatataq llamk'an?
 Kay k'itiqa qhawarin maypichus mousepa kursoqnin kachkan hinaspa mayqen llimp'itaq chay urapi kachkan. Chay willakuykunataqa utqaylla huk virtual platomanchá apachin, chay platonqa computadormanta pantallapa hatun kayninman hinallataqmi. Sapa kuti mouse kuyuptinqa, chay k'itiqa huk pixelta llimp'in, chay llimp'iqa kursoqpa uranpi kaqmi, maypichus kursoq kachkarqa chaypi. Kayqa ninanmi, pantallapi llamk'aspa hinaspa kuyuchispa, platomanta llimp'iyoq puriykuna paqarimun. Kay puriykunaqa sapaqmi, hinaspa usuario computadormanta llamk'asqanta rikuchin.
 
¿Imataq pasan unay tiempo pasayta?
 Sichus kay ruwayqa unayta purin chayqa, platonqa hunt'ayta qallarin hinaspa puriykunaqa manaña allintachu rikukun. Aswanpas, platonqa huk mana yuyayniyoq qapariq (ruido) hinam rikukun. Ichaqa, kachkanmi pantallapi wakin k'itikuna, chaykunaqa manam unay tiempo pasaytaqa anchata tikrakunchu. Chayqa kanman, ahinataq, Windows sistemapa uray patan, maypichus softwarepa icononkuna kachkan. Chay k'itikunaqa platomantaqa chaynallataqmi qhipakun.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Tukuy
 Kay k'itiqa teknologiamanta hinaspa artemanta huk huñunakuy, chaymi yanapan sapa p'unchay computadormanta llamk'ayta huk rikuy munayman t'ikray. Ñawpaqtaqa computadormanta llamk'ayqa sapa kuti ruway hinalla rikukunman, ichaqa kay proyectoqa rikuchin chay ruwaypas kikin k'achayniyoq hinaspa takiyuq kasqanta.
 
Pukyu k'iti
 Kay proyectopaq Python k'itiqa kaypim kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Yuraqkunamanta qillqakuna
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zaznamy-rostlin/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2023-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zaznamy-rostlin.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zaznamy-rostlin/index.html
============================================================
Yuraqkunamanta qillqakuna
 
 inti raymi killa 2023 
 
 
 
Qallariy
 Kay qillqapim qawarisunchik huk llamkayta, chaymi tupan yuraqkunap kawsayninwan, antañiqiqwan, kamachiywan (programación) hinallataq kawsay rikuy arte nisqawan. Kay llamkayqa Arduino nisqatam Python nisqawanpas llamk'achin yuraqkunap kawsayninmanta rikuykunata ruwanapaq.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | arduino tupanakuyninpa siq'in]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Imaynatataq kayqa llamk'an?
 Arduinoqa kimsayuq tupuykunayuqmi (sensores) – q'uñita tupuy, k'anchay tupuy, hinallataq allpapi yakuta tupuy. Kay tupuykunaqa t'ika p'ukukunapim kachkan, achka laya yuraqkunawan (ahinataq: patizones, arwi, inti t'ika, berenjenas, mastuerzo, ch'illi, hukkunapas). Tupuykunaqa sapa kutim q'uñimanta, k'anchaymanta, hinallataq allpapi yakumanta willakuykunata huñun.
 Programataqmi ruwan ch'usaq siq'ina p'anqata 100×100 pixelniyuqta. Sapa pixel kay siq'ina p'anqapiqa tupuykunamanta chaskisqa willakuykunaman hinam llimp'isqa kachkan. Pixelpa RGB llimp'inqa kayhinam: R (puka) q'uñiman tupan, G (q'omer) k'anchayman, hinallataq B (anqas) yakuman.
 
Huk p'unchawpa rikuykuna
 Ñawpaqtaqa qawarisunchik kay llamkaypa sapaqlla kaqninta, maypichus huk siq'i huk p'unchaw yuraqpa kawsayninta rikuchin. Programataq qallarin huk ñiqin pixelta llimp'ispa paña hawa kuchupi, hinaspataq sapa pixelta llimp'in tupuykunamanta chaskisqa willakuykunaman hina. Huk pixel llimp'isqa kaptinqa, programataq suyan huk ratu qatiqnin pixelman manaraq rispa. Huk siq'i tukunayaptinqa, qatiqnin siq'imanmi rin hinaspataq llimp'iyta qatiylla qatin. Kay llamkayqa kachkan llapa pixelkuna llimp'isqa kanankama.
 
Inti t'ikapa qillqasqan:
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]

Lluqsiyninkunata huñuy
 Huk p'unchawpa rikuykuna ruwayta tukuspaqa, kay llamkayta mast'arisunmanmi achka siq'ikunata ruwanapaq, chaykunaqa unay pachamanta willakuykunatam rikuchin.
 Sapa t'uqu llimp'isqakunataqa uray-uraymanmi churaykuna, chaywanmi hatun siq'ikuna paqarimun. Huk lluqsiynin tukuytawan qatiqnin qallarisqanrayku, pacha kawsayqa manam p'akikunchu. Kayhinam ruwasunman 100x500 pixelniyuq siq'ikunata, pichqa lluqsiymanta huñusqa kaptinqa.
 
Inti t'ikapa qillqasqan:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-9.jpg]

Tupuykuna chawpipi unayachiy
 Sapa pixel tupuy chawpipi unayachiytaqa mast'arichwanmi. Kayhinam huk t'uqu format 100×100 pixelniyuqqa manam huk p'unchawllatachu rikuchinman, aswanqa achka p'unchawkunatapas. Kay yuyayqa yanapawanchikmi yuraqpa wiñaynin qatiyta unay pachapi huk siq'illapi.

Yuyaykunata tupanachiy
 Iskaynin yuyaykunata tupanachispaqa, achka siq'ikunatam ruwasunman, maypichus sapa siq'i achka p'unchawkunata rikuchin, hinaspataq llapa siq'ikuna achka p'unchawkunata utaq simanakunatapas qawachin. Kayhinam yuraqpa unay pacha wiñayninta qawarisunman.

Lluqsiyninkunata sut'inchay
 Lluqsiq siq'ikunaqa yuraqkunap kawsayninmanta sapaq rikuytam quwanchik. Anqas “siq'ikuna” siq'ipiqa tutakunatam rikuchin, chaypim yakulla karqa, manataq q'uñichu, hinallataq manam k'anchay karqachu. Chaymanta, qillu “siq'ikunaqa” paqarimun p'unchaw tukuyta inti q'uñichisqanraykum, chaymi q'uñita hinallataq k'anchayta yaparqan. Kayqa siq'ipi rikukun puka hinallataq q'omer llimp'ikuna yapakusqanraykum, chaykuna huñusqataq qillu llimp'ita ruwan. Wakin kutipiqa tuta qallariypi pisilla siq'ikunatam rikunki, chayqa wasipi k'anchayta hapichisqayraykum. Huk lluqsiyninkunapitaq, k'anchay pisiyasqanrayku yachanchikmi ahinataq para chayamusqanta.
 
Pukyu qillqa (Código fuente)
 Kay llamkaypaq Python qillqaqa kaypim kachkan:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Tukuy
 Kay llamkayqa allin rikuchiymi imaynatachus antañiqiqwan kawsay pachapi kaqkunata allinta hamut'ayta atinchik. Lluqsiq siq'ikunaqa yuraqkunap kawsayninmanta hinallataq imayna kawsayninmanta rikuytam quwanchik.
 
Yuyaykunaqa allinmi, kay llamkayqa yuraqkunap kawsayninmanta sapaq rikuyta quwaptinchikpas, manam llapa imayna kawsasqankutachu rikuchin. Yuraqkunaqa achka laya willakuykunatam chaskinku, ahinataq wayra yakuyay, pacha muyuy, allpapi imayna kaqkuna, k'anchay kallpa, hinallataq imayna k'irisqankupas. Kay llamkayqa manam yuraqpa llapa kawsaynintachu qillqayta munan, aswanqa kimsayuq hatun tupuykunallatam, chaykunataqa atinmi tupuyta hinallataq rikuchiyta.
 
Manam yachanchikchu imaynatachus yuraqkuna kay willakuykunata hamut'anku, ichaqa yachanchikmi paykunaqa chayman hina kawsasqankuta. Kay llamkayqa yuraqkunap kawsayninta huk formatoman tikraymi, chayta aswan allinta hamut'ananchikpaq.

TITLE: MS Word nisqapi kutichiy (rekurze)
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
MS Word nisqapi kutichiy (rekurze)
 
 ayriway killa 2022 
 
 
 
Microsoft Word nisqapi kutichiy (rekurze) ruwayta tarirqani. Iskay imaynan chaypaqqa necesitakun: t'inkiy (propojení) ruwaywan tablawan. T'inkiy ruwaywanqa A qillqap kaqninkunata B qillqaman churasunman, chayrayku A qillqapi imatapas tikrachispaqa, B qillqapipas kikinmanta tikrakunqa. Huk rimaypiqa, B qillqaqa A qillqamanta willan. Kutichiytaqa qallarisunman kikinmanta willaq qillqata ruwaspa. ¿Imaynatataq chayta ruwasunman? A qillqata ruway (sutichasun doc A.docx) hinaspa B qillqata (doc B.docx), A qillqapi ch'usaq tablata ruway, chayta iskaychay hinaspa B qillqaman t'inkisqata churay. Chay musuq tablata B qillqamanta iskaychay hinaspa A qillqapi kaq tablaman t'inkisqata churay. Chaynapim tabla kikinmanta kikinman churakuyta qallarin. Chaymantaqa MS Word pisi-pisimanta sayaykun hinaspa tukupayninpiqa wichqakun. Chay ruwaytaqa qatiq videopi qawayta atinki.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Llullu harawi

LANGUAGE: qu

SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Llullu harawi
 
 ayriway killa 2022 
 
 
 
Kay qatiq harawipi llapa rimaykuna (yuyayninchikman hina, yanapakuq rimaykunata mana yupaykuspa) llullu rimaykunam; chaykunaqa rimaykuna kanku, chaykunaqa cheqchua simipa fonotaktikanman hina kanman karqan – chay qillqakunaqa chay simipi kanmanmi – ichaqa manam imatapas niyta munanchu, manataqmi llamk'achikunchu. Chay harawiqa manam imatapas niyta munanchu, chaymi ñawiriqpa yuyayninmantam kachkan imayna entiendey, chaywanpas wakin llullu rimaykunapa sunqunpi kachkaq qillqakunaqa anchatam yuyayta k'uchichin.
 
Kay ruwayqa manam munarqanchu sumaq harawita ruwayta, aswanqa mamallaqta siminchikpa atiykuna k'uchichiyllatam munarqan. Kusikunapaqmi, sapa llullu rimaypa imayna kasqanta, imayna k'askasqantapas allinta riqsiy atikun.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Harawi mana imatapas niyta munaptinpas, ñawiriqpa yuyayninpiqa imapas rikurimunmanmi. Munarqanchikmi yachayta, imaynatam yuyaymananman karqan yachayniyuq antañiqiq (AI). Chayraykum wakin AI modelokunata llamk'achirqanchik (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion, hinallataq Night Café), chaykunaqa qillqasqanchikman hina rikch'aykunata ruwayta atinku. Sapa modeloqa hukniraymi, wakinqa cheqap rikch'aykunata ruwanku, wakinqa astawanmi mana riqsisqa rikch'aykunata ruwanku, wakinñataqmi sumaq k'achapaq hina. Chaymantapas, manam llapankuchu cheqchua simita chaynallata entiendinku (ahinataq, ru-DALL-E modeloqa anchatam cheqchua simita entiendin).
 
Kay harawipa sapa rakiyninkunatam AI modelokunaman qusqa karqan, sapa modelotaqmi kikinpa yuyayninman hina rikch'aykunata ruwarqan. Sapa serie ruwasqanchikmantam huk videota ruwarqanchik, chaypim rikch'aykuna rikurimun, chaymantapas chay harawi rakiykunaqa ñawirikusqam kachkan. Deep Daze modelotaqmi yuyaykuna imayna ruwakusqanta qillqayta atin, chaymi videopi rikunanchikman imayna yuyaykuna pisi-pisimanta ruwakusqanta.

TITLE: Allpa pachapi kawsaypa pisichasqa wiñay kawsaynin
SLUG: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi.mdx
BUILT_HTML: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/index.html
============================================================
Allpa pachapi kawsaypa pisichasqa wiñay kawsaynin
 
 pawqar waray killa 2021 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/1R8M4nRLPWQ]
 
 
Kay kuyuq rikch'aqa yachay wasipi llamk'ay hinan ruwakurqan Plzeň llaqtapi yachay wasi qhatuymanta, k'acha kawsaymanta, kamaymanta (Nerudova 33).
 
Kay kuyuq rikch'aq huk rikch'ayninmi t'ikraykun 200 000 watakunata Allpa pachap wiñay kawsayninmanta. Chayqa Homo sapiens rikch'aqpa kawsasqan pachamanmi tupan. Chayraykun runaqa kay kuyuq rikch'aqpa qhipaq kaq rikch'ayninllapi rikukun.
 
Llapa rikch'aykuna kay kuyuq rikch'aqpi llamk'achisqaqa kikin ruwaqpa hap'isqanmi karqan.

============================================================
TITLE: Hromnické quchapi kawsay
SLUG: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/index.html
============================================================
Hromnické quchapi kawsay
 
 qayna killa 2021 
 
 
 

[MediaObject: https://www.google.com/maps/embed?pb=!1m18!1m12!1m3!1d2873.387394621076!2d13.444731496763541!3d49.85061064085366!2m3!1f0!2f0!3f0!3m2!1i1024!2i768!4f13.1!3m3!1m2!1s0x0%3A0x93e249730f9d76f5!2zSHJvbW5pY2vDqSBqZXrDrXJrbw!5e1!3m2!1scs!2scz!4v1609929799218!5m2!1scs!2scz]

 
Hromnické quchapi [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko] kawsayqa Plzeň-sever suyupi kachkan, chayqa kawsay pachamanta yuyayniyuqmi. Ñawpaq pachapi rumi q'illayta hurqusqankurayku, quchapi yakuqa aslla asidom (chay pHqa 2.6 – 2.9 kachkan). Chayraykum achka qillqakunapi ninku: „yakuqa mana kawsayniyuqmi“. Chay kamachiy qillqapipas [https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d0/Tabule_Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko.JPG] nillantaq: „… algaskunamanta qipamanqa [yakuqa] mana kawsayniyuqmi“. Ñuqaqa chayta mana chiqapchu nini, kawsayqa imaymanamanmi yachayta atin. Chayraykum chayman rirqani kikin qhawamunaypaq.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg | Hromnické qucha]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg | tupuy pH]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg] 
 
19. killa 2020 punchawpi Hromnické quchamanta yakuta hurqurqani. Achka kawsayniyuq kawsaykunaqa rikukullarqanmi, ch'aqu yaku ch'inlla kaptin. Pakomár k'uychi k'uychikunatam tarirqani.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg | pakomár k'uychi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg] 
 
Chaymantapas huk ch'iwi k'uychitapas tarirqanim, chaytam mikroskopwan riqsirqani.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg | ch'iwi k'uychi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Huk kawsayniyuq kawsaykunatapas tarirqanim, kay iskay laya krásnoočko kawsaykunaqa qucha hawanpi q'umir q'aytu hina rikukullarqanmi.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg | krásnoočko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg | krásnoočko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/aoPDRJ_XaxI]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg | krásnoočko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/XbHSxjzpSYE]
 
 
Chay rikuykunapiqa prokaryotické kawsaykunatapas rikunmi – sinice (uchuy k'aspichasqa kawsaykuna). Huk prokaryotické kawsaytawanmi tarirqani, chayqa kay bakteriam (manaraqmi allinta riqsini).
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg | bakteria]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg] 
 
Iskay simana qhawasqaymantam nálevníky kawsaykunata tarirqani. Ñawpaq kaqqa vířenka, iskay kaqtaq uroleptus.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg] 
 
 
Ancha kusikunapaqmi chayna asidoyuq yakupi kawsaykuna kawsasqanqa. Chayraykum nini, yakuqa manam ch'inllachu nispanku. Allinmi kanman chay kamachiy qillqata musuqyachiy, chaymantapas internetpi qillqakunatapas. Ichaqa manam chayllachu Hromnické quchapi kawsaykunaqa, chayraykum allin kanman wakin killakunapi yaku tupuyniy, chaymantapas ukhumanta yakuta hurquy (chay ukhupiqa mana samayniyuq, mana kanchayniyuq kawsaykunapas kanmanmi).

Yuyay fantasiap saywayninmanta
 
 ayamarq'ay killa 2020 
 
 
 
Kay qillqaqa yachay wasipi yachaqpa ruwasqanmi (t'aqwiy: yuyay) mayqin yuyaymantapas, Plzeň llaqtapi Střední odborná škola obchodu, užitého umění a designu yachay wasipi yachachkaptiy.
