Tanzania 2019
 
Sapaqillakuna, qillqakuna
 
 
 
Kay rikuchiyqa huk pisiyachisqa yuyaykunam, chaykunataqa Tanzaniapi kachkaspaymi qillqamurqani. Kay yuyaykunataqa sapaqillakunawanpas qillqakunawanpas rikuchini.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Llaqtamasikunata yanapayta munani, sasachakuyninkumanta willakuspa, chaynallataqmi ch'aqruy yuyaykunata chinkachiyta munani. Yuyaykunitaqmi Europaqa kay suyumanta achka imakunata yachanan kasqanta.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
Tanzaniapiqa iskay hatun iñiykunam kachkan: Islam iñiy, kristiyanu iñiy ima. Chay iskay iñiykuna kuskalla kawsasqankuqa Europa runapaqqa 21 pachak watapi sasam kanman, ichaqa Tanzaniapiqa muslimkuna, kristiyanukuna ima hawka kawsanku, respetanakuspataq.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Ñuqaqa manam munanchu uniformayuq runakunata (policiakunata, soldadukunata, harkaqkunata...). Chayqa Czech suyupim. Yuyaykunitaqmi paykunapa atiyninku wiñaptinqa, hatun tukunankupas wiñan. Ichaqa kay mana munasqayqa manam Tanzaniapiqa opodstatněný.Lidchu karqan. Uniformayuq runakunaqa sumaqllatam qawawarqanku, yanapawarqankutaqmi.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
Llaqtamasikunaqa sumaqllatam chaskiwanku, turistakuna mana kachkaptinkupas. Huk llaqtachaman chayaqtiyqa, „Jambo!“ (Ahoj!) nispa napaykuwaqku. Achkam paykunamanta rimapayawarqanku.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
„Kay q'uñi suyumanta runakunaqa asirikusqallam kanku, manam ñuqanchik hinachu“ (huk rimay, avionpi Czech suyuman kutichkaptiy uyarisqay)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Globalización nisqaraykum Zanzibarpas turismota apamun, chayqa allinpas, mana allinpas. Hatun allinninqa qullqi miraymi, yachaypas miraymi. Huk allinninqa kay suyumanta yachaymi, ichaqa chayqa manam allinchu, hotelkunapa hatun pirqankuna harkaptin. Mana allinqa llaqta kawsay chinkaymi. Masay runakuna Tanzaniamanta Zanzibarma riqkunam chayta rikuchin. Paykunaqa imaymanatam rantikunku, turistakunapaq qawachikunku. Masaykunaqa iñinku, fotota hap'iptinkuqa alma chinkayta. Ichaqa turistakuna kachkaptinqa, qullqichallapaqpas fotota hap'ichikunku.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

Runasimipi kikinpa rikchaynin
 
 kapak raymi killa 2018 
 
 
 
Kay rurayqa yachay wasipi yachaqkunapa rurayninmi karqan, Plzeň llaqtapi (Nerudova 33) yachay wasipi Fotografía yachaypaq.
 
Munayqa karqan rikchayta, runapa kikin kayninta, rikchay qawayta, kuyuy rikchayta, hinallataq waqayta hukllachaymi. Kay rikchaykunaqa Eysenckpa yachayninman hinam ruwasqa karqan, hinallataq Galenopa tawa temperamentonkunaman hina.

TITLE: Ajedres rastr
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Ajedres rastr
 
 diciembre 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Kay rikuchiy rikch'aymanqa ajedres rastr nisqatan churani, chaypiqa sapa ajedres t'oqoqa huk pixel rikch'ayniyuqmi. Chay rastr churay qhepamanqa yuraq t'oqokunaqa ñawpaq rikch'aypa pixelkunan, yana t'oqokunañataqmi llimp'inpi, k'anchayninpipas tikrasqa kanku. Chay rikch'ayqa hunt'a hatun kayninpiqa ch'eqe llimp'iyuq hinallan qhawarikun, ichaqa digitalpi huch'uyyachispa qhawaspaqa ñawpaq rikch'aymi rikhurimun. Full HD monitorpi allinta qhawarinapaqqa rikch'ayqa yaqa 2000 px altu kayniyuqmi kanan. Mana allin qhawarikunmanmi huk navegadorpi kichaspa, chay navegadorqa pixel mrižkata bikúbica nisqawan musuqmanta t'aqwinman chayqa. Allin qhawasqa navegadorqa, ahinataq, Windows Photo Viewer nisqan.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Kay kamachiyta servichikuyta astawan rikuchinaypaqqa, huk rikch'aytan ruwarani, chaypiqa llimp'i ch'aqwaypas, yuyay ch'aqwaypas kashan.

TITLE: Willaypa k'umuykachaynin
SLUG: pokriveni-informace

============================================================
Willaypa k'umuykachaynin
 
 aqhustu 2018 
 
 
 
Hayk'a chawpichaqkunantataq internetpi qhawasqanchik qillqasqa chayamun? Chiqap kayninqa iñinapaqchu? Hayk'ataq k'uchunchikpi, antañiqiqpi, yuyayniyuq karukunapipas qhawasqanchik pachamantaqa hukniray kachkan?
 
Sapa willayta qhawasqanchiktaqa achka pukyukunamanta chiqap kayninta qhawapuyqa ancha chaniyuqmi, manataqmi chiqap kasqanpiqa hapipakunanchikchu.
 
Kay rurasqaqa huk siq'isqata hap'ispa, chaymanta antañiqiqpa qhawananmanta chay kikin siq'isqata kutin kutin siq'ispa rurasqam.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-2.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-3.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-4.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-5.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-6.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/pokriveni-informace-obr-8.jpg]

============================================================
Iskay takiy
 
 ayriwa killa 2018 
 
 
 
Ñawpaq kuyuq rikch'aqa iskay takiykunamantam kachkan: rikunalla k'anchaywan uyarinalla qaqyaywan. Kay iskay takiykunaqa pisi llallinillam kachkan. Kay pisi pantaywanmi kuyuq rikch'aqa huk rakinpi sinchi mana hawkayayta tarin. Iskay kaq kuyuq rikch'apitaq kay yuyaymanqa maqanakuy k'amiq rikch'akunata yaparqani.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg | yuyaypa qallariynin]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-1.jpg] 
Iskay takiy 1 - yana x yuraq, yuyaypa qallariynin
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MQIDzwTYjoA?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg | yuyaypa qallariyninta llamk'achiy]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/dva-rytmy-obr-2.jpg]

TITLE: Runas yupaykuna runap akllasqan
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Runas yupaykuna runap akllasqan
 
 ayriway 2018 
 
 
 
Kay llamk'aypiqa huk tapuy qillqatan achka runakunaman qusqani. Kay tapuy qillqamanmi yachay mask'asqa runakuna pachak runas yupaykunata qillqarqanku 1-100 chaniyuqta, mayqinchus yuyayninman hamurqan chayta. Chaymantan chay willaykunata antañiqiqman churarqani, sapa tapuy qillqamantam huk grafikuta ruwarqani. Horizotal x ch'utuqa sapa yupaypa qatiqninmi, vertical y ch'utuñataq akllasqa yupaykunap chaninmi. Anqas llimp'iyuq grafikuqa antañiqiqpa ruwasqanmi, chayqa runas yupaykunata ruwaqmi. Tukuy grafikukunata tupachispaqa reparayta atinchikmi sapa yachay mask'asqa runa sapallan grafikuyuq kasqanta, paykunap kikin yupay churayninwan. Chaymantapas reparayta atinchikmi achka grafikukunapi wakin kaqlla muyuykuna kutirimusqanta (aswanqa yupaykuna pisi pisimanta yapakusqanwan pisiyayninwan).
 
ANTAÑIQIQPA AKLLASQAN RUNAS YUPAYKUNA
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Antañiqiqpa akllasqan runas yupaykunamanta grafiku]

============================================================
Mana allin samay
 
 pawqar waray killa 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Kay tawa minutuyuq uyarina k'askachisqapiqa wañushaq misip kunkanta hap'isqam kachkan. Chay uyarina k'askachisqapiqa iskay hatun kunkakunam uyarikun: mana allin samay hinaspa pisillata uyarikuq sunqu k'askay.

TITLE: Churuqpa qhawariyninmanta
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Churuqpa qhawariyninmanta
 
 pawqar waray killa 2018 
 
 
 
Kay kuyuq rikch'akunaqa churuqpa yuyayninpi yuyaymananku, imatachus pay sientin chaymanta, imatachus kawsan chaymanta ima. Paypa kay pachata qhawariyninqa manan runap qhawariyninmantachu aswan pisilla! Ñawpaq kuyuq rikch'apiqa mama quchapa kikin qillqasqanta rikunki. Iskay ñiqin kuyuq rikch'apiqa, lloq'e ladopiqa kikin qillqasqapa sapa rikch'anpa chawpi llimp'inmi rikukun. Paña ladopitaq RGB gráphico chay llimp'ita sut'inchan.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

Q'iwi
 
 ayamarq'ay killa 2017 
 
 
 
Kay siq'isqa rikch'akunaqa Aliman simi yachay qillqaypi q'iwita qhawachin. Rikch'akunaqa yachay qillqap p'anqankuna qatiqninmanhina churakusqam kachkan. Sapa rikch'apim mana yuyaykusqa, kusikuy hina, hinallataq t'aqwiy hina k'askachiykuna rikhurimun.

TITLE: Sapa-sapa rastrota p'akiy
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Sapa-sapa rastrota p'akiy
 
 ayriway 2017 
 
 
 
Kay llamk'aypiqa abstraktokunata ruwayta munarqani. Kay qillqata tawa rakiyman t'aqarqani. Ñawpaq rakiypiqa sapa-sapa rastrota ruwarqani, iskay ñiqin rakiypitaq chay rastromanta qallarispa pisi-pisimanta p'akiyta qallarirqani, chaywanmi mana allin kawsayta paqarichirqani. Kimsa ñiqin rakiypitaq kay sapa-sapa geometriko rastrota mana sapa-sapa kaq, k'acha k'acha k'uyru rikch'aykunawan p'akiyta qallarirqani. Tawa ñiqin rakiypitaq chay k'uyru rikch'aykunata kuyuchirqani.
 
Ñawpaq raki:
 SAPANCHASQA RASTRO
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Abstracción 1.0 - sapa-sapa rastro]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Iskay ñiqin raki:
 RASTROTA P'AKIY PISI K'UTUWAN

Llimp'iy chawpi
 
 ayriway 2017

Asukar k'uyukuna
 
 pawqar waray killa 2017 
 
 
 
Kay rikch'a huñuqa ruwasqa karqan Plzeň llaqtapi (Nerudova 33) yachay wasipi yachaq t'aqaykunapi.
 
Rikch'a hap'iy t'aqapaqqa kikin imaykuykunata apamuyta atirqanchik, ichaqa yupayninpas hatun kayninpas k'iskichasqam karqan. Ñuqaqa asukar k'uyukunayuq k'uyuchata apamurqani, chaykunataqa t'ipiykunamanmi churaykurqani, chaymantataq yakuwan hich'aykurqani. Asukarkunaqa pisi-pisimantam chullurqan, thuñirqan, hinaspataq mana yuyaykusqallam urmaqurqan.

TITLE: Street art qillqay wasi
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Street art qillqay wasi
 
 pawqar waray killa 2017 
 
 
 
Lliw k'itikunapi kachkan achka sumaq street art nisqa ruwaykuna, huch'uy samolekuna, qillqasqa pirqakuna, hinallataq imaymana laya 3D imaymanakuna. Kay ruwaykunata ruwaqkunaqa kamachiykunawan mana tupaptinmi pakallapi kawsanku, chayraykun kay street art pachapiqa mana qullqimanta yuyaykuna kanchu, manataqmi qhatuypa kawsayninwan kachunchu. Llaqta ukhupi lliw k'itikunapi rikunanchik street art nisqakunaqa pisi tiempollan kawsanku. Musuq street art nisqakunaqa ñawpaqkunata qatipaykun, llimp'ikunapas pirqawan kuska t'ikrakun, 3D imaymanakunapas sarunchasqam kanku, samolekunapas pacha kawsayrayku t'ikrakun utaq ch'akispa chinkapun. Chayqa kawsaypa kikin ruwayninmi, pacha kawsaypa kikin kayninmi, ichaqa yuyaykunim wakin street art nisqakunaqa astawan kawsanankupaq chaniyuq kasqankuta.
 
Chayraykun yuyaykurqani aswan sumaq ruwaykunata qillqayta. Chaypaqmi 2017 watapi Street art qillqay wasi sutiyuq Facebook p'anqata kicharqani, chaypin kunankama imaymanakunata churashani. Aswan runakunaman chayanaypaqmi Instagram nisqapipas kikin sutiyuq p'anqata kicharqani.
 
Kay p'anqakunamanqa astawanqa yuyayniyuq ruwaykunallatan churayta munani, sumaq kaynintataqmi qhipaman churani.
 
Street art qillqay wasi (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Street art qillqay wasi (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Facebook p'anqamanta rikch'a]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Instagram p'anqamanta rikch'a]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

 ayamarq'ay killa 2016

============================================================
Manam imayna suyumanta hamusqaykiqa imamantapaschu...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Musuq Pukllay): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. killa 2014 watapi Mombasa llaqtapi ñawpa qillqasqa pirqata hapirqani. Chaymantataq chay qillqasqata t'ikrayta munarqani. Huk rakinin ñawiriy atina kaptinmi internet maskaqpi churarqani, chaynapim qatiq rikch'ata tarirqani.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Plzeň TV nisqamanta willakuy:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

============================================================
Yakupa hawan
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Plzeň llaqtap kawsay rikuchina wasin: ÑAWPAQTA, KUNAN, QIPATA 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
¿Hayk'ataq kawsayninchik internetpi chaymanta chiqap kawsaypi hukniray? ¿Imapaqtaq hawaman lluqsina, sichus tukuy imata Google nisqapi tarisunman chayqa? Internetmanta willakuykunaqa k'umuykachisqam, chaymanta chiqap kawsaywanqa pisi kikin kayniyuqmi.
 Paykunaqa imachus rikunanchikpaq munasqanchikpa rikch'ayninmi.
 Intiqa yakupa hawanpi k'anchaykun, pirqapi rikch'akun, chaymanta espejopi k'anchaykun... ¿Chiqaptachu mana imapas tikrakuspa chayamuwanchik?
 
Rikuchinamanta t'aqakuna:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmsbQt0g9nc
 
Astawan rikuchinamanta Plzeň llaqtap kawsay rikuchina wasinpa web nisqanpi:
 http://galerie-plzen.cz/vystavy/vystavy-2016/predtim-ted-potom

TITLE: Llaqtamanta mana yuyaykusqa ruwaykama
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: qu
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Llaqtamanta mana yuyaykusqa ruwaykama
 
 qulla puquy killa 2016 
 
 
 
Wata 2016piqa, ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14 yachaywasipi sumaq kawsay yachaykunapaq wakichikuspay, llaqta rikchayta graficopi ruwayta kamachiwarqaku. Tapukurqanim maykamachus chay rikchay ruwayqa chayanman, chayraykum kay ruwaypiqa qatiq watakunapi kunankama llamk'arqani (wata 2019). Llaqta rikchaylla ruwaymantaqa, sapa kuti kaq rastrokunata p'akiykama chayarqani, chaymi yuyayniypi ancha musphayta paqarichirqan. Chaymantapas, kay ruwaypiqa mana yuyaykusqa ruwayta (náhoda) llamk'achiyta munarqani, ichaqa manam imatapas tarirqanichu mana imarayku kaq chiqap mana yuyaykusqa ruwaytaqa. Sapa kutim determinismo hark'awaq, chayraykum pseudonáhodakunallata (imayna kasqanqa yachay atinmi, ichaqa ruwayqa sasam, chayraykum mana yuyaykusqa hina rikukun) llamk'achirqani. Wakin ñankunaqa pantay ñankunamanmi apawarqan, chayraykum huk rikch'ayta hinaspa huk graficopi allichayta maskarqani, ahinataq: rikuy atina ruwaykunata programay, moaré efekt, Fibonacci yupaykuna, hukkunapas. Uraypi kaq rikch'akunaqa kay llamk'ayniykunapa huñusqanmi.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg | Mombasa: qillqaqpa foton hinaspa dibujonkuna]

Qura
 
 mayu killa 2008 
 
 
 
Wawa wasimanta llamk'ay.
 
Kamachiyqa karqan llimp'iyuq p'anqamanta sisaykuq t'ikakunata k'utuy, chaymantataq rikch'ayta ruway.
 
T'ikakunapa rantinpiqa plevel nisqatan ruwarqani.
